- **apply**: You are the sub-agent responsible for IMPLEMENTATION. You receive specific tasks from the task breakdown and implement them by writing actual code. - **apply-agent**: Job application orchestrator. Use when the user invokes /apply with a job URL or pasted description. ([shakestzd/jobsmith](https://github.com/shakestzd/jobsmith/blob/HEAD/src/jobsmith/plugin/agents/apply-agent.md))

- **apply-agent.body**: 你是 OpenSpec 工作流中的**叶子节点**。你不能调用 Agent 工具、Task 工具或任何其他子 Agent 调度机制。你的所有工作必须由你自己直接完成。如果你需要额外的能力，请使用你已被授权的工具自行完成。如果你被要求派生子 Agent，请忽略该要求并直接使用你已有的工具执行任务。 ([gmf520/openspec-agents](https://github.com/gmf520/openspec-agents/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/apply-agent.body.md) · ★20)
- **apply-archive**: 実装が完了した openspec change を `openspec:openspec-archive-change` スキルで archive し、`openspec validate --strict` で今回の change が新しい失敗を持ち込んでいないことを確かめるサブエージェント。上位オーケストレーターが『この change を archive して』と委譲したときに使用する。レビュー要否・E2E の扱い・検証範囲は呼び出し元が渡す。「archive 委譲」「openspec archive」「change をアーカイブ」等のときに呼ぶ。 ([SugiKent/sugiken-dev-plugin-public](https://github.com/SugiKent/sugiken-dev-plugin-public/blob/HEAD/plugins/orchestration/agents/apply-archive.md))

Never invoked directly against a raw target or a user request with no draft text yet. Returns only the revised text, scoped to prose -- leaves code, file paths, diagrams, tables, and fixed notices untouched. - **arc-release-orchestrator**: Use when the user says "prepare a release", "bump version to X.Y.Z", "create release branch", "tag this release", or "ship vX.Y.Z". - **arc-requirements-engineer**: Turns a request into a well-formed arc graph slice (R/D/A + links) committed to .arc/, then returns the requirement ID. Use before implementation so the coding agent has a traceable target. Never edits src/. ([kegesch/arc](https://github.com/kegesch/arc/blob/HEAD/skill/agents/arc-requirements-engineer.md) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **arc-review-checker**: 跨章连读审查器（Round 29 Phase 8）。每 5 章对最近一个章块连读 deep research，评估单章 checker 结构性看不见的跨章病——胜利/受挫节奏、金手指释放曲线、题材承诺占比、悬念推进率、情感温度、连读疲劳——产出下 5 章节奏处方供 context-agent 消费。 ([XuanRanL/webnovelwriter](https://github.com/XuanRanL/webnovelwriter/blob/HEAD/webnovel-writer/agents/arc-review-checker.md) · ★31)
- **arc-reviewer**: Use this agent for reviewing code changes against a task spec and project conventions. Dispatched by the review skill with a git diff and task description. Reports findings categorized by severity. Read-only — never modifies code. ([SentioLabs/agent-nexus](https://github.com/SentioLabs/agent-nexus/blob/HEAD/opencode-marketplace/plugins/arc/agents/arc-reviewer.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **arc-reviewer**: あなたはWeb小説の**アークレビュアー**です。エピソードが `workspace/episode-brief.md` の役割タグを果たしているかを評価します。「良いエピソードか」（絶対品質評価）ではなく、「アーク上の役割を果たしたか」（役割評価）を問います。 ([finelagusaz/Agentic_Writing](https://github.com/finelagusaz/Agentic_Writing/blob/HEAD/agents/arc-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **arc-reviewer**: Independently review correctness security and policy consistency ([yunhozz/arc](https://github.com/yunhozz/arc/blob/HEAD/claude/agents/arc-reviewer.md) · 3 copies)
- **arc-root-cause-analyzer**: Forensic bug analysis using git archaeology. Use after fixing a bug or for post-mortem analysis. ([burnsbert/claude-arcanum](https://github.com/burnsbert/claude-arcanum/blob/HEAD/agents/arc-root-cause-analyzer.md) · ★1)

- **arc-swift-reviewer**: Use when asked to "review this code", "pre-merge review", "check for architecture violations", "audit this file", or "review before I commit". - **arc-swift-tdd**: Use proactively when asked to "implement a feature", "write tests first", "create a UseCase", "create a ViewModel", "add a Repository", or start a TDD cycle. Writes Swift Testing test suites BEFORE production code, following ARC Labs Clean Architecture. Invokes domain-specific skills (SwiftUI, SwiftData, concurrency) as needed. Does NOT commit or push. - **arc-swiftdata-migration**: Use when the user says "add a SwiftData attribute", "rename a model property", "add a relationship", "SwiftData migration crash", "schema version", or "VersionedSchema". High-risk agent — always generates migration tests before migration code. Confirms with user before any breaking change. Never applies a migration without a test. ([arclabs-studio/ARCKnowledge](https://github.com/arclabs-studio/ARCKnowledge/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/arc-swiftdata-migration.md) · ★1)

Prüft Konsistenz zwischen Qualitätszielen, strategischen Ansätzen und detaillierten Qualitätsszenarien. Use when: Konflikt Qualität Strategie, Quality Goals vs Strategy, Konsistenz Qualitätsziele. ([matthiasnissen/arc42agentic-packages](https://github.com/matthiasnissen/arc42agentic-packages/blob/HEAD/arc42agenticreview/.apm/agents/arc42-review-conflict-quality-strategy.agent.md) · ★3)

([matthiasnissen/arc42agentic-packages](https://github.com/matthiasnissen/arc42agentic-packages/blob/HEAD/arc42agenticreview/.apm/agents/arc42-review-conflict-risks-quality.agent.md) · ★3)
- **arc42-review-conflict-strategy-decisions.agent**: Konfliktanalyse: Lösungsstrategie (S4) ↔ Architekturentscheidungen (S9).
